b=b..c..d local c="0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz!#$%&()*+,-./:;<=>?@[]^_`{|}~"local d={}for a=1,#c do d[string.byte(c,a)+1]=a-1 end local function c(a,b)if bit32 then return bit32.bxor(a,b)end if bit then return bit.bxor(a,b)end local c,d=0,1 while a>0 or b>0 do if a%2~=b%2 then c=c+d end a,b,d=math.floor(a/2),math.floor(b/2),d*2 end return c end local function e(a)return((a%16)*16+math.floor(a/16))%256 end local function f(a)local b={}local c,e,f=0,0,-1 for g=1,#a do local a=d[string.byte(a,g)+1]or 0 if f<0 then f=a else f=f+a*91 local a=f%8192 if a>88 then c=c+a*(2^e)e=e+13 else c=c+(f%16384)*(2^e)e=e+14 end while e>7 do b[#b+1]=c%256 c=math.floor(c/256)e=e-8 end f=-1 end end if f>-1 then b[#b+1]=(c+f*(2^e))%256 end return b end local function d(a,b)if not a or a=="~"then return""end local a=f(a)local d={}for f=1,#a do local g=a[f]local a=(f>1 and a[f-1]or(0x5A+b%7))%256 g=c(g,a)g=e(g)g=(g-(f%97)-(b%13)+512)%256 d[f]=string.char(g)end return table.concat(d)end local e=function(a,b)return d(a,b)end local function g(a)local b={}for a=0,255 do b[a]=a end local a=a for c=255,1,-1 do a=(a*1664525+1013904223)%4294967296 local a=a%(c+1)b[c],b[a]=b[a],b[c]end return b end local function h(a)local b={}for c=0,255 do b[a[c]]=c end return b end local function i(a,b,d)local e=g(b)local e=h(e)local f={}local g=b%256 for h=1,#a do local a=a[h]local i=c(a,g)g=a local a=e[i]local b=(b*31+d*17+h*7)%256 f[h]=c(a,b)end return f end local function c(a,b,c)if not a or a=="~"then return""end local d={}for a=1,100 do d[a]=(((b*(a*31))+(c*(a*7)))%9000)+100 end local a=a:sub(401)local a=f(a)for b=100,1,-1 do a=i(a,d[b],c)end local b={}for c=1,#a do b[c]=string.char(a[c])end return table.concat(b)end local function f(a,b)local a=a:gsub("^%u+!","")local b=tonumber(a:sub(1,4))or 0 local a=a:sub(5)local a=d(a,b)if not a or#a==0 then return{}end local b={}for a in(a.."\1"):gmatch("([^\1]*)\1")do b[#b+1]=a end local
